Backtests/opencode/run-20261121-184931/#56783

Issue #56783 Merged fix PR #58313 Run 21 Nov 2026 · run-20261121-184931 anomalyco/opencode

V2: compaction fires on first request / loops when compaction.buffer >= model context limit

Better

Our patch was judged better than the merged fix.

In this judgment, Patch B = our agent, Patch A = the merged fix.

Correctness: the issue asks for three things: clamp an oversized buffer, don't compact when there is no older history, and stop the internal 'Nothing to compact yet' StepFailedError from surfacing. Patch A only addresses the first, and only partially: it ignores the buffer when buffer >= window, but a buffer just below the window (e.g. 90k on a 100k model) still yields a near-zero ceiling and compaction on every step, and it introduces a cliff where buffer 99,999 gives a 1-token ceiling while 100,000 gives the default reserve. Patch B caps the buffer at half of each model's window, which handles the issue's '400k buffer meant for a 1M model' case uniformly across all smaller models, and additionally guards both the auto-compaction due check and the background precompute path so that a session with nothing written yet skips rather than entering compaction and raising StepFailedError. That directly resolves the second and third asks in the issue. Tests: both add tests that exercise the oversized-buffer case; B additionally adds a config-plugin-level test with buffer 600000 on a 100k window and a test that an over-ceiling empty-history context returns 'skipped' instead of failing, covering the 'Nothing to compact yet' path. The one removed test line in B is a necessary fixture change (adding text content so the new no-history guard doesn't short-circuit existing tests). The independent tests were MISSING on all trees, so they provide no signal. Scope: B carries a large bun.lock reshuffle that is unrelated to the fix and is a mild risk, but it does not obscure the code change and does not outweigh A leaving two of the three reported behaviors unfixed and the clamp itself incomplete. Craft: B's named constant with explanatory comment and updated docstring are consistent with the surrounding RESERVE_* conventions; A's one-line condition is clean but the 'ignored' semantics are a less principled policy than a proportional cap.

Four judges ruled on this ticket in parallel, and the verdict comes from counting their votes. Each saw the issue and the two patches, labelled A and B in random order (two of them saw ours as A), with issue links and version numbers removed. Where tests could check the fix, they also saw the results of tests a separate agent wrote for the issue, run on both patches. None was told which patch was ours. The reasoning above is one judge's, shown verbatim.

The issue

View on GitHub

Summary

With compaction.buffer set to a value greater than or equal to the model's context limit, V2 starts automatic compaction on the very first request and re-triggers it on every turn. This emits meaningless structured summaries at session start and sometimes surfaces an internal Session.StepFailedError: Nothing to compact yet.

Environment

  • opencode v2.0.32 (Linux x64; also confirmed config is read by V1 1.19.0)
  • Session model: opencode/mimo-v2.6-flash-free

Reproduction

opencode.json:

{
  "compaction": {
    "auto": true,
    "buffer": 600000,
    "keep": { "tokens": 15000 }
  }
}
  1. Start a V2 session (any directory with an AGENTS.md).
  2. Send a single first message.
  3. Observe the message stream: a compaction message is created immediately after the user message (reason: "auto"), summarizing an empty history, and repeats every 1-2 turns.

Startup log line:

ERROR "Failed to drain Session" cause="Session.StepFailedError: Nothing to compact yet
    at SessionRunner.runStep
    at SessionRunner.drain
    at ServerProcess.start"

Expected

  • An oversized buffer should be clamped to < context limit (or rejected with a clear warning at config load).
  • Compaction should not run when there is no older history to compact.
  • The internal StepFailedError should not surface as a user-facing failure at server start.

Actual

The trigger is estimated_tokens > context_limit - max(requested_output_tokens, buffer) (docs state buffer default = 10% of the limit). When buffer >= context_limit, the right-hand side is negative, so the condition is always true and compaction fires on every step, including the first. Because buffer is a single global value evaluated against each model's own limit, a value chosen for a large-context model (e.g. 400000 for a 1M model) breaks every smaller model.

Additional context / feature request

buffer and keep.tokens are only configurable globally in the top-level compaction block; per-model settings.compaction only accepts type: native|summary. This forces a single compaction policy across models with very different context windows. It would help to allow a per-model (or per-provider) buffer, and/or support an absolute "compact at N tokens" threshold, so users can pick different compaction points for a 200k model vs a 1M model without a global value that is unsafe for one of them.
